*It’s been getting pretty warm lately as summer seems to have arrived in full swing. I’ll check on the clothes again in an hour or so and they should be dry.*

Madeline:  "So Lucky goes down. The bottle is now spinning up in the air. Beans goes to grab it but he’s still holding the jar of nuts so now that’s wobbling in his hands."

*She would murder you if you tried to call her that.*

*So now she’s admitting to snooping. What happened to “I was looking for a pen”?*
*Tomorrow, I’m going through my desk and removing anything that could be used against me.*
Cynthia:  "Hey, Maddie. What’s your nickname there?"
*Madeline crossed her arms and pouted.*
Madeline:  "Chuckles called me a klutz and now everyone does. I wanted something cuter."

*She started shuffling through my movie collection and I sat down next to Cynthia, ready to see what crazy thing she pulled out.*

*Huh. Not as bad as I thought it’d be.*

Madeline:  "I’m so excited to watch this."

*Cynthia pouted at being told to shush.*
*And so we wasted away the afternoon watching movies. Cynthia couldn’t seem to fully relax around Madeline but that was probably because Madeline was so loud, getting into the movie and almost reenacting the fight scenes.*
*By the time the second movie was playing, it was becoming a war to control the TV volume. Cynthia had also tried to tie her up into the blanket burrito, but Madeline saw it as a game and fought back.*
*It all came to an end when there was a knock at the door.*
You:  "Looks like we’ve pissed off the neighbours."

*I crossed the apartment to the door and opened it to a startled looking girl.*

Miyu:  "Oh nooooo. I made her sick, didn’t I?"
*The answer was yes but you obviously didn’t just say that to people so I awkwardly shrugged instead.*
Miyu:  "Ooo. I’m sorry. Can I come in and talk to her?"

*I moved aside so Miyu could enter.*
*She hurried over to Cynthia and Madeline gave her a weird look.*

Miyu:  "...I mean. It seems that way."

*Madeline was in full goofy mode while Cynthia tried to reel her in. Miyu watched on nervously but I could tell she was having fun. She would even chime in with a word or two.*

Cynthia:  "<USER>, what are you doing?"

*I brought her back closer to me and smiled.*
*If you still want me here, I’m ok with it. I hold onto you as long as you need me too.*
*Towels: check. Lotion: check. Phone: off.*
*Alright. Everything was looking good for our little trip to the beach.*
*After Cynthia’s run with a cold, it was my turn to be sick. I did my best to hide it so she wouldn’t make a big fuss, but she still found out in the end.*
*With everyone feeling healthy now, we could finally join Miyu at her family’s summer house and relax. Of course, work wasn’t too happy about me taking more time off, but I hadn’t used any vacation time in years so there wasn’t anything they could do about it.*
Cynthia:  "I’m almost done."
You:  "We’re still good on time so don’t rush."
*I dropped my bag by the cooler at the door and started searching through the cupboards for a granola bar or something.*
Cynthia:  "Breakfast wasn’t enough for you?"

Cynthia:  "<USER>! Were you going to forget about the meat?!"

Cynthia:  "Yup. Let’s not keep Maddie waiting any longer."
Cynthia:  "Who knows what trouble she’s already gotten into."

*It was an absolutely gorgeous day with barely any clouds in the sky. Perfect weather for a beach trip. I could only hope that this weather extended to the country. Though I’m sure Miyu would have called if anything was wrong.*
*We wandered the streets in silence until the dreaded sight of a klutzy catgirl came into view. She smiled brightly and hurried over to us, tripping at the last second and crashing into Cynthia.*
Madeline:  "Ah!"

*Cynthia quickly turned back to the kitchen.*
Cynthia:  "That’s another thing I should apologise for. Having another maid come into your house and point out the flaws of your current maid must have been embarrassing for you."
You:  "No. Because you’re not do-"
Cynthia:  "You have my assurance that I will correct this behavior for you in the future."
*I sighed and rubbed my head. What was this? Why was she acting this way?*
You:  "Enough of that! What’s going to happen in a week?! What’s this about you being the next queen?!"
*Cynthia frantically looked to the clock and started to panic.*
Cynthia:  "Oh, <USER>, you have to go! You can’t be late for work."
You:  "It’ll be fi-"
Cynthia:  "No no. It’s important that you take your work seriously. You have a responsibility to your boss..."
*I groaned as she started up with this load of nonsense*
You:  "That’s not important right now!"
*She ignored me as she grabbed a bag and handed it to me.*
Cynthia:  "Here’s a special lunch I made for you."
*She started shoving me towards the door. It was useless to fight against her right now. I didn't have enough time to get her to drop that act and talk to me normally. I quickly put on my shoes and let her push me out the door.*
Cynthia:  "H-Have a good day at work, <USER>. I’ll have everything taken care of by the time you get home."
You:  "We’re going to talk about this more when I get home."
*She smiled nervously and closed the door. I sighed and stared at the door for a moment.*
*The facade, the door... It felt like she was trying to put up as many barriers as she could to keep me away.*
You:  "I can’t help you if you don’t let me in."
*The door responded with silence, but that was what I expected. What a mess this was turning into it. What the hell was I supposed to do now?*
*I started walking away. I had a long day ahead of me.*
You:  "I can’t help you if you don’t let me in."
*Cynthia pressed her forehead to the door and closed her eyes as she heard the footsteps getting further away.*
Cynthia:  "I’m sorry, <USER>. Just...give me more time."
*She knew it was foolish to try and pretend that nothing had changed between them. But still, she tried her hardest to maintain the illusion this morning and here was the end result.*
*Everything had changed and there was no going back. She was the last member of the royal family and in less than a week, she was going back to her world.*
*She looked around the apartment and felt her chest tighten at the thought of leaving it. But what choice did she have? Her people were waiting for her. Could she honestly abandon them and live a normal life?*
*Any happiness or relief of seeing Angelica alive again had been replaced by dread.*
Cynthia:  "If only I didn’t ask Maddie to search for her. Then I could..."
*She didn’t finish. It was just another line of reasoning to escape the problem she was in instead of dealing with it.*
*She did, however, find it odd that Madeline wasn’t with Angelica. If she had found her, shouldn’t she have visited last night as well?*
*The next chance Cynthia got to ask Madeline, she would, but for now, she had the apartment to deal with.*
*If she could clean it properly to Angelica’s specifications, she would feel a little more accomplished and clear headed. Then she could find a solution to her... other problem.*
*She sighed again, feeling guilty as she kept referring to her world as a “problem”.*
Cynthia:  "I’m making sure the apartment is perfect so when I leave, <USER> isn’t burdened with a messy place."
Cynthia:  "..."
*That sounded worse and she felt guilty again at the idea of just abandoning <USER>. There just wasn’t a proper way to think of this situation that wouldn’t leave her feeling conflicted.*
Cynthia:  "Focus on what you can control then."
*She clapped her cheeks to give herself a jolt and looked over the apartment nervously.*
Cynthia:  "25 areas that need cleaning! Ha! I could do 100 no problem!"
*She said it but still hadn’t taken a step forward.*
Cynthia:  "Yup. There won’t be any problem I can’t handle. This will be easy."
*The apartment waited in anticipation as she gathered all her strength and courage and forced herself to take the first step.*
*I watched my computer monitor intensely. All around me the sounds of the office just felt like a monotone drone. My mind just couldn’t focus on anything that wasn’t Cynthia related.*
*What was she doing right now? Was it ok to leave her all alone? I should have just called in sick to work.*
*Rrr... All this damn noise was making it hard to think.*
Dan:  "Are you listening to me?"
*I looked up at Dan and, no doubt, my face didn’t tell a pretty story as he seemed to hesitate for a second before continuing.*
Dan:  "You look like shit."
You:  "Thanks, Dan."
Dan:  "See, this is what I’m talking about. Bossman isn’t too happy with your attitude of late. You better keep your head down."
*What have I done? I only just started working today. How can my attitude be a problem after 30 secs?*
You:  "Why’s he upset?"
Dan:  "All the time off you’ve been taking off, ignoring the phone calls, the..."
*Dan panicked as he looked towards the boss’ office.*
Dan:  "Oh shit. Here he comes. Good luck."
*He scooted off as fast as he could and I tried to make my face look a little more normal before the boss showed up.*
*Sure enough, he slowly made his way over to me, looking me up and down once.*
Boss:  "You look like shit."
You:  "Thanks."
Boss:  "Come to my office for a moment, would you?"
*He started walking, not waiting for me to get up. Looks like I should have listened to Dan a little better. I might have had more time to hide.*
*I stood up and followed my boss, the other employees staring at me like I was on death row or something. Anytime I made eye contact with them, they would quickly look away.*
*We entered his office and he made his way around his desk and sat down with a groan. He motioned lazily towards one of the chairs in front of his desk.*
Boss:  "Close the door and sit."
*I followed his instructions and he took a moment to flip through some files before looking at me.*
Boss:  "I like you, Chuckles. I really do."
*Why didn’t you use my real name then? Did you forget it?*
Boss:  "You do your job. You keep your head down and we can always count on you."
*“To do everyone’s job for them” wasn’t said but you could feel it lingering in the air.*
Boss:  "But lately... I’ve been hearing some troubling reports."
*I actually have a spine?*
Boss:  "You’ve been taking time off work more frequently and the other team members have been reporting that they can’t get ahold of you when they need you."
*Shouldn’t the whole office be called into here for that?*
Boss:  "Like I said, I like you. I have you on the shortlist of people I would want to promote to manager."
*The carrot you’re trying to dangle in front of me is old and moldy. Besides, this was the first I’ve ever heard of any type of job opening.*
Boss:  "But based on these reports... It’s making me wonder if I was incorrect in my assessment of you."
You:  "I’m sorry."
*That was pretty much all you could say in these situations. It was all they wanted to hear anyhow. It didn’t matter if you meant it or not.*
Boss:  "However, an opportunity has presented itself that could help turn your situation around."
*I gave him a confused look.*
Boss:  "Our branch up north is struggling to meet its deadlines. Usually we’d send Lukas up there but his doctor still hasn’t cleared him from the burn ward. A tragic accident."
*Yes... “Accident”. An image of a laughing Madeline entered my mind.*
Boss:  "So I put your name forward as his replacement. You do this, it will look really good in the president’s eyes. That promotion could be as good as yours."
*He smiled like it was a good thing and I should be grateful. I smiled too and hoped my face showed the correct emotion.*
You:  "Thank you, sir."
Boss:  "You leave next Monday and-"
*Next Monday... That would be the day after Angelica’s ultimatum and when Cynthia returned to her world...*
Boss:  "Are you ok?"
*Shit. I must have shown my true emotion there for a second.*
You:  "Yes, sir."
Boss:  "-dry chuckle- You looked a little overwhelmed there for a second."
You:  "J-Just grateful at the opportunity."
*He slid a file across his desk to me.*
Boss:  "Your ticket and everything you need to know about the project is there. Submit a report to me this Friday with your action plan and call me everyday while you’re there to report the day’s event."
You:  "Yes, sir."
Boss:  "Make me proud... You can go back to your desk."
You:  "Thank you, sir."
*I quietly left the office with the file in hand. It looked like it finally happened. I had become the office burden.*
*It was joked among us that the only reason Lukas was sent on these trips was because he was so disruptive that the boss was just trying to get rid of him.*
*To think that I was the next disruptive person in line, especially seeing as how big a problem the office klutz could be... It almost made me proud.*
*I guess I was more done with this place’s bullshit than I had thought.*
*More stares followed me as I returned to my desk and I hadn’t even sat down yet when I felt a presence slowly descending upon me. I should have figured thinking about her would only summon her.*
Madeline:  "-giggles- I heard you were getting in trouble. What did you do?"
You:  "Took time off for being sick. Didn’t pick up my phone once or twice. The usual."
*Madeline’s face twisted in disgust.*
Madeline:  "That’s worthy of getting yelled at?"
You:  "Apparently. And it wasn’t yelling as much as listening to someone trying to guilt trip you into doing more work."
Madeline:  "Good luck with that."
You:  "Right?"
*Madeline studied my face curiously.*
Madeline:  "You don’t sound too sad about being called in the boss’s office so why do you look like shit?"
You:  "That’s becoming a pretty popular expression today."
Madeline:  "Don’t look like that if you’re sick of hearing it then."
You:  "Fair enough. Just a little problem with Cynthia this morning."
Madeline:  "If you hurt her..."
*The shift in her body language and tone would have been enough to scare even the most hardened of people. Lucky for me, I wasn’t intimidated by this clown.*
You:  "Relax. It wasn’t me. It was this Angelica chick that came by yesterday. She was kind of a bitch and..."
*I stopped because Madeline was frozen in place with a wide eyed look. That...wasn’t what I was expecting from her.*
*I was starting to think that I should just throw all my expectations out the window today because I was just wrong as fuck about everything.*
You:  "What’s wrong?"
Madeline:  "She’s alive?!"
You:  "Cynthia had the same reaction. What’s up?"
*Madeline went quiet as she brought her hand up to her mouth. She looked terrified for a second before quickly leaving to find a chair and wheeling it over to me.*
*She sat down and pulled herself in so close to me that I needed to lean back and put a little distance between us.*
Madeline:  "You’re not lying to me, are you? This woman called herself Angelica and Cynthia confirmed it?"
You:  "Yeah."
*She studied my face carefully before sighing.*
Madeline:  "Start from the beginning and don’t spare the details."
*I told her everything that happened last night and this morning. Madeline leaned back and groaned.*
Madeline:  "Forgive the expression but the cat is out of the bag now, isn’t it? I can’t believe she would share that information with you so willingly."
You:  "It’s really got Cynthia all messed up."
Madeline:  "Yeah, she had been doing her best to keep it from you."
You:  "Why though? I wouldn’t have cared."
Madeline:  "Once you find out someone’s royalty, it’s hard to treat them normally. She was probably just enjoying being treated as no one special."
You:  "Yeah, and now she’s going to leave in less than a week."
Madeline:  "Yeah, but she couldn’t pretend to be your maid forever. Something like this was bound to happen eventually."
You:  "Yeah, but..."
*I stopped as Madeline gave me a curious look.*
Madeline:  "But..."
*I looked away and she sighed.*
Madeline:  "You’re in love with her, aren’t you?"
Madeline:  "-sigh- You stupid son of a bitch."
*She looked away and groaned.*
Madeline:  "That explains her..."
*She closed her eyes for a second and took another deep breath before opening them and staring directly into my eyes.*
Madeline:  "So, what are we going to do then?"
You:  "You’re going to help me?"
Madeline:  "I’m going to help her. That’s my job."
Madeline:  "Now, have you said anything to her?"
You:  "Who is she? And why were you both so surprised that she’s still alive?"
*Madeline thought for a second before sighing again.*
Madeline:  "I guess I can’t use the excuse of protecting our world’s information when she’s willing to break the rules so easily."
Madeline:  "Angelica is Cyn- Her Highness’ primary caregiver and personal maid. She and I don’t see eye to eye on a few things when it comes to the princess’ well being."
Madeline:  "As you would expect, a royal family doesn’t really raise their own kids so the queen assigned Angelica to be head maid for Princess Cynthia and she’s been responsible for educating and, essentially, mothering her since her birth."
Madeline:  "This would be fine, except Angelica is... Well, you saw yesterday, she’s just so cold and professional."
Madeline:  "She raised Princess Cynthia on a super strict schedule, trying to make sure she grew up to “honour the family name” and all that load of shit."
You:  "It’s really weird to be hearing the title “princess” before Cynthia."
Madeline:  "It was much weirder for me to be stuck calling her just Cynthia. It never felt right without title."
You:  "What was your job there? You said you worked for her mom, the queen I’m guessing, and Angelica referred to you as “Lady”. I assume you are a knight of such."
Madeline:  "Yeah. I’m a member of the Order of the Rose. A group of knights in service to the royal family."
You:  "That sounds way too fancy for you considering what it’s like working with you."
Madeline:  "I’ll let that slide because if the roles were reversed... I’d probably say that same thing about you."
You:  "Thanks."
Madeline:  "Yeah. No problem."
You:  "So if Cynthia was raised strictly... I think I can understand why you took on the role you did of big sister."
*She nodded.*
Madeline:  "Exactly, what else could I do? My first week I was there... I was just shocked to watch this young girl walk around like a husk."
Madeline:  "I was the youngest member of the Order so I felt I had the best chance to relate to her."
Madeline:  "Of course I was in my early twenties and she was in her early teen years so...relate was a bit of a stretch but we still connected over some stuff. "
Madeline:  "We talked books at first and I saw that she was itching to go on an adventure herself so I snuck her out one day to go to the market with me."
You:  "I can’t imagine that went well."
Madeline:  "-laughs- Nope. Thankfully, it wasn’t Angelica that found us returning but Kris, the Captain of our Order. Princess Cynthia stood up for me so I wasn’t dismissed but still disciplined severely. Didn’t change anything though. Princess Cynthia and I only got closer."
Madeline:  "-giggles- Of course that just pissed off Angelica but it was too late, once I saw the light return to Princess Cynthia’s eyes... I fell in love and promised to do everything I could to protect it."
Madeline:  "That bitch wasn’t going to stop me. I was now Princess Cynthia’s big sister."
Madeline:  "No matter where she went or what she did, I was always there to watch over and protect her because she means more to me than anything else in life."
*She moved in real close again, getting right up into my face.*
Madeline:  "If you can’t begin to match my feelings of love for her then just give up now. I’m not going to stand by and watch someone hurt her."
*She looked me in the eyes and nodded.*
Madeline:  "Good. There might be hope for you yet, Chuckles."
*She looked me in the eyes but shook her head.*
Madeline:  "If you want me to believe it, you better try harder than that."
You:  "I will prove it."
Madeline:  "You better."
*Her little stare down over with, Madeline leaned back in her chair again and crossed her arms, taking a moment to collect her thoughts.*
Madeline:  "So yeah. As you can imagine, Angelica and I butted heads a few times. She would try to make Princess Cynthia’s schedule stricter but I would always find some way to make sure the princess had time for fun."
You:  "Explains why a lot of Cynthia’s stories start with “Maddie dragged me out to...”."
Madeline:  "-giggles- Yup. I wanted to show her the world. Wasn’t expecting to show her two worlds though. I’ll take that as a bonus."
*I think credit for showing her this world belonged more to me but I didn’t want her to get too distracted by it. There was still an important question that needed to be asked.*
You:  "And why did you two think Angelica was dead?"
Madeline:  "-sighs- The attack on the castle was chaos. Obviously, the Order of the Rose did everything in its power to get the royal family out but... The only one we could get out...was...Princess Cynthia. "
Madeline:  "...And even then it came at a cost."
Madeline:  "We lost many from our Order as we fought from room to room. In one room, we were outnumbered horribly. Angelica and the maids decided to stay behind so the last of the Order could make a break for the door."
*She shook her head.*
Madeline:  "Nobody should have survived that. There were too many of them."
You:  "And yet."
Madeline:  "Yeah. She got out."
You:  "Tough girl."
Madeline:  "Tougher than I thought."
You:  "What about the rest of your Order?"
*She shook her head again and looked down sadly.*
Madeline:  "I’m all that’s left."
You:  "I’m sorry."
Madeline:  "Thanks. When we got outside, we were closely followed by the peasants. We tried to fight them off but Princess Cynthia must have panicked or something because she took off. I was ordered to chase after her while the others held the line."
Madeline:  "I don’t know where she ran off to. All the trails went cold."
Madeline:  "...I could only assume the worse."
Madeline:  "It was a couple of weeks before I chased a rumour to the portal between our worlds."
You:  "And that brought you here."
Madeline:  "Yeah. That brought me here."
You:  "Yeah. I can see why you were so surprised to hear she was alive."
Madeline:  "Princess Cynthia had asked me to investigate and find any word of her and there was nothing."
You:  "That’s strange."
Madeline:  "Very. Like I mean nothing. My contact couldn’t find a single mention of her anywhere."
You:  "Do you think she hid out in this world?"
Madeline:  "Maybe, but then again, my contact would have said something like when Princess Cynthia crossed over."
*She was tapping her arm intensely as the wheels in her mind spun.*
Madeline:  "I don’t like this. I’m going to look into it more and get some answers. If she was still alive, I should have heard something. And something that’s bugging me is how’d she find out where you two lived in the first place."
*Good question.*
You:  "I’d like to know that information too."
Madeline:  "I’ll check that out. In the meantime, it sounds like Princess Cynthia is going to need some help sorting out her feelings. Angelica always has a way of making her revert back to that husk mode. Try to break her out of it."
*I wouldn’t really call what Cynthia was this morning a husk. She just seemed to be doing everything she could to forget what happened but I understood what Madeline was saying.*
You:  "Leave it to me."
Madeline:  "Remember, if she’s too closed off, just do what I do to snap her out of it. Challenge her to something. She can’t resist it."
*She didn’t need to tell me that. I’d done it enough times it was becoming my favourite hobby.*
You:  "She’s so easy that way."
Madeline:  "-giggles- Right? She always has something to prove, that girl."
*She looked sad for a second before quickly standing up.*
Madeline:  "I should get back to work..."
*Never thought I’d hear her say that.*
Madeline:  "...Take care of her, will you?"
You:  "Of course. Keep me informed with anything you find out."
Madeline:  "I will."
*She took the chair away from my desk and returned it to its spot before walking away without another word.*
*I watched her go before feeling the presence of another approach my desk. It was just Dan coming back to ask about the boss.*
Dan:  "What’s going on between you and Klutz. You two are always so buddy-buddy with each other."
You:  "She wanted to know the same thing you did."
*I held up the file the boss gave me.*
You:  "They’re shipping me away up north. Lukas isn’t out of the burn ward yet."
Dan:  "So you’re the next office burden, huh? -laughs- Congratulations."
You:  "Thanks. Couldn’t have done it without you."
*Dan gave me a look before dryly laughing.*
Dan:  "Oh, Chuckles... Have fun up there."
You:  "I will."
*He hurried off to tell everyone about my punishment and I shoved the file aside. I had more important things to work on.*
*I stood outside the door and tried to listen in. My hand was shaking as I reached for the doorknob so I stopped to try and regain my composure.*
*The apartment was quiet... Uncomfortably so as there were usually the sounds of her making dinner or tidying up. My mind raced as it tried to explain it.*
*Did she run away? Was she hurt? Or was she simply napping?*
*I finally grabbed the doorknob and opened the door.*
*I stepped through the frame and waited a moment, but no one came to greet me at the door. I peeked my head into the kitchen and it was empty. In fact, it looked like nothing had changed since I left this morning.*
*I quickly took off my shoes and entered the kitchen fully. There, I saw it.*
*DVDs were scattered everywhere, the sofa was moved with the cushions taken off, the desk was a mess as it seemed to be stacked with everything that was around the TV and in the middle of it all, sat an overwhelmed, sad catgirl.*
*It was clear that Angelica’s comments had gotten to her as she probably tore the house apart looking for those “25 areas that still needed cleaning” or whatever bullshit Angelica went on about.*
*Feeling the presence of another in the room, Cynthia slowly looked up from the ground to meet my eyes. Her eyes were red and puffy and a streak of tears could still be seen on her cheek.*
*In an instant, she flipped, putting on a smile and trying to seem cheerful.*
Cynthia:  "Welcome home, <USER>. Forgive me, I was just trying to get a little cleaning done and lost track of time. I’ll get dinner started right away."
*My heart felt heavy in my chest as she got to her feet and turned around to hide her face.*
Cynthia:  "Don’t mind the mess. I’ll have it all straightened up before we go to bed tonight."
*She wiped away the tears in her eyes before turning around and keeping her head down as she started towards the kitchen.*
Cynthia:  "Was there anything you wanted for di-"
*As she tried to walk past me, I stopped her by putting my hand on her shoulder. She seemed to shrink away from my touch for a second before slowly turning to me.*
Cynthia:  "Yes, <USER>?"
*Without a word, I slowly wrapped my arms around her and pulled her in close to me, hugging her with everything I had. She didn’t flinch or try to fight it. She just stood there trembling slightly as I heard her breathing get a little ragged.*
Cynthia:  "I-Is som-"
You:  "Cynthia..."
*She froze at my voice, remaining quiet for a couple long seconds.*
Cynthia:  "Y-Yes?"
You:  "What are you doing?"
Cynthia:  "I-I..."
*She sniffed a couple of times before resting her head on my shoulder and wrapping her arms around me.*
Cynthia:  "I don’t know..."
Cynthia:  "-sighs- I don’t know what to do right now."
Cynthia:  "What should I do?"
You:  "Well..."
*This was the part I usually sucked at. If I could answer that, my life would be completely different right now.*
You:  "Let’s start by putting everything back in its place. Once the apartment feels a little more organised, we’ll be able to think a little more clearly."
Cynthia:  "...Ok."
*I tried to let go of her but she continued to hold onto me.*
Cynthia:  "-sniffs- One more minute, please."
You:  "Ok."
*She hugged me tightly for a little while longer before pulling back. Her eyes were still a little red but she wasn’t crying anymore. More importantly, she wasn’t looking away. She met my eyes and wasn’t trying to hide behind a mask.*
You:  "You ready?"
*She nodded.*
You:  "Well..."
*I scanned over the mess, trying to find somewhere to be able to start.*
You:  "Let’s push the couch back into place first to give us more room."
*Between the two of us, we pushed the couch back easily and got the cushions back on it.*
You:  "Why’d you move the couch out of place?"
Cynthia:  "I thought one of the spots that Angelica was talking about was behind the sofa."
You:  "If it was, she has some crazy vision being able to see that from the doorway."
*I laughed, shaking my head at how ridiculous it was but Cynthia remained serious.*
Cynthia:  "Wouldn’t be the first time she’s seen something like that."
*I just looked at her stunned.*
You:  "Really?! What type of superpower is that?"
Cynthia:  "The one that forces you to clean your room again after you’ve already cleaned it the first time."
You:  "Sounds lovely."
Cynthia:  "It’s important to make sure your living space is as clean as possible at all times."
*I gave her a look of disbelief as she headed for the desk. It sounded like she was quoting someone there. Which made me wonder if that was what she truly believed.*
*I didn’t ask it out loud though. The way Madeline had talked, Cynthia was fiercely devoted to Angelica so trying to question it wasn’t going to help Cynthia right now. I needed to focus on just getting her smiling again.*
Cynthia:  "Between the two of us, we’ll finally be able to dust the TV stand."
You:  "Is that why you pulled everything out?"
Cynthia:  "Yeah. I normally just dust in between but I figured I would give it a really good dusting this time."
You:  "But then everything went everywhere."
Cynthia:  "-nervous laughter- Yeah. Once I pulled everything out, I tried to organise it but then I noticed a stain on the ground so I went to clean that..."
Cynthia:  "-nervous laughter- Yeah..."
*I nodded understandingly.*
You:  "Well, let’s just put everything back in then. That’ll make things seem a little less overwhelming around here."
Cynthia:  "We already have everything out. Might as well deal with it now."
Cynthia:  "If you’re going to do the job, you might as well give 110 percent right?"
*She had a point that it was already all out. I figured she would just want to get everything organised quickly though so she didn’t have to deal with this anymore.*
*I said I’d help though so...*
You:  "Alright. Hand me something to wipe it with."
*She handed me the cloth and I gave all the shelves a good wipe down to remove all the dust. Considering how clean she usually keeps things, it was a pretty easy task.*
*She inspected it and nodded in approval.*
Cynthia:  "Good. I think it would pass any inspection at this point."
*Organising all the DVDs and games took a little bit of time but thankfully, it was both of us doing it. If it was just her doing it, she probably would have been here all night.*
You:  "Ok. Next... You said there was a stain?"
*She pointed to a spot on the floor and I had to really get close to it to make out a tiny little stain. It was probably from some soup or a split drink from who knows how long ago.*
You:  "Oh yeah. Good eye picking that up."
Cynthia:  "It wasn’t easy to find. I really had to check everything closely."
*Then you probably didn’t need to put that much work into it.*
Cynthia:  "I figured it was time to mop the floor anyhow so let’s do the full area."
You:  "I’ll pull the table and chair out of the way then."
*I dragged away the table and wheeled out the office chair while Cynthia started mopping. She scrubbed away at a few spots that must’ve had stains on them before looking over everything and nodding.*
Cynthia:  "That’s better. A stain in the house is a stain on your honour."
*With everything we cleaned, Cynthia seemed to have some Angelica quote lined up for it. Who knows how long Angelica had been beating those lines into her head. This behavior must be what Madeline was referring to when she called it “husk mode”.*
*As far as I could tell, there wasn’t anything too wrong with it. Was Madeline overreacting to it? Or did she just disagree with Angelica so wholeheartedly that she lost sight of who Cynthia had become?*
*Her relationships with Angelica and Madeline were starting to make sense to me now. The two of them were essentially pulling Cynthia in opposite directions, trying to tell her who she was supposed to be.*
*It seemed like she had never been asked who she actually wanted to be...*
*Wait. Maybe there was one time and she wasn’t asked so much as she made the decision herself.*
*She wanted to become my maid.*
*At the time, it was strange to me but now, I think I understand it a little.*
*She was a lost princess, forced out of her world and without those two’s guidance. In the brief moment, she was free to make any decision she wanted and from that, she chose this life.*
*When given another opportunity to be allowed to choose... What life would she actually choose for herself?*
*She looked at me questioningly, probably able to read my expression.*
You:  "Are you going to go back?"
*It was silent for a second before we were interrupted by a DVD falling out of the TV stand.*
*She turned around and picked it up, looking at it quietly before putting it back.*
*It was the one question that needed to be asked but the one we both dreaded to have to deal with. It was going to come up eventually though so there was no point wasting time.*
Cynthia:  "I don’t know."
You:  "I don’t want you to go back"
*She turned to face me again, her face unreadable.*
Cynthia:  "I know."
*It wasn’t a response I was expecting and my heart grew heavy at what it meant.*
You:  "...But you have to."
*She looked away again, the sadness clearly visible on her face.*
Cynthia:  "I feel like there’s no choice."
You:  "There’s always a choice."
Cynthia:  "Not when the lives of millions are involved."
*I scratched my head and started pacing. I had no words to that.*
*The silence between us grew heavy and she picked up the mop and bucket.*
Cynthia:  "I haven’t made a final decision yet so... Let’s just make dinner and we’ll talk more about it later."
*I reluctantly nodded. It would give me more time to think of some way to convince her to stay.*
*Hopefully.*
*The food tasted like ash as we slowly ate our dinner in silence. I was sure there was nothing wrong with how the food was prepared, except for the parts I had a hand in, but it still didn’t help any of the flavour come through.*
*Cynthia’s eyes never rose from the plate in front of her as any conversation between us just ended up tittering off into silence again.*
*Preparing the food had been equally as awkward but she would still ask the occasional small talk question. Any answer I had would just be met with her agreeing before going quiet again.*
*Even if it wasn’t going anywhere, I could tell Cynthia was still trying to keep us talking. She wasn’t hiding away in a shell. I needed to find a way to keep something going between us for her.*
You:  "So, ummm... Madeline did something weird today."
*Cynthia smiled a little.*
Cynthia:  "She always gets into something, doesn’t she?"
You:  "Surprisingly she was pretty good today that way. It looked like she was actually working pretty hard today."
Cynthia:  "She probably just found a new game on the internet."
You:  "Yeah, probably."
Cynthia:  "...So what’d she do that was weird then?"
You:  "She talked about herself and your world."
*Cynthia gave a curious look and it slowly dawned on me that this probably wasn’t the best conversation topic right now.*
*The fact that Madeline talked about Cynthia’s past might make Cynthia clam up a bit and go quiet again.*
You:  "I-It wasn’t much, mind you. It just makes sense now why Angelica called her Lady Madeline."
*Oh shit. I probably shouldn’t be saying that name. I should stop now.*
You:  "Speaking of Angelica..."
*What? No! I said stop.*
You:  "Madeline was as surprised as you were to hear she was alive."
*Oh God. Why am I wording it that way? Just stop, you stupid idiot.*
You:  "-nervous laughter- She stared at me like her eyes were going to pop out of her sockets."
*I dropped my fork and covered my face in my hands hoping it would finally shut me up.*
You:  "I’m sorry! I shouldn’t be talking about that."
*Cynthia giggled softly and I split my fingers to see her smiling at me.*
Cynthia:  "It’s ok. It’s...not exactly something we can pretend isn’t happening."
*I removed my hands from my face and she put her fork down and pushed her plate away.*
Cynthia:  "So... Maddie didn’t know..."
You:  "Yeah... It sounded like those two didn’t get along very well."
Cynthia:  "Not really no. Angelica was very particular about things and Maddie is... Maddie."
You:  "Chaos incarnate, yes."
Cynthia:  "They always seemed to be at each other’s throats."
You:  "Sounded fun to grow up around."
*She shook her head.*
Cynthia:  "I tried to stay out of it. More times than not, Kris would have to step in and keep the peace."
You:  "Nothing like the royal guard fighting with the maids, huh?"
*She smirked for a second but went back to looking gloomy.*
You:  "-sighs- Look..."
You:  "I don’t care about what you were born as. Whether it be royalty or... A pear."
You:  "It doesn’t change our time together or who you are."
Cynthia:  "-sighs- I know... I knew you would say something like that but still... I couldn’t bring myself to tell you the truth."
Cynthia:  "I hid it because I didn’t know you and wanted to make sure you wouldn’t try and hurt me but...after that... It was just nice pretending I wasn’t the princess."
*She laughed dryly.*
Cynthia:  "Here I was lecturing you about being more responsible with work and cleaning up after yourself. Meanwhile..."
*I shook my head.*
You:  "They’re not the same. You can’t think you're hypocritical because you wanted me to pick up my socks while you’re trying to forget that your kingdom kicked you out."
You:  "Two completely different feelings there. -laughs-"
*She laughed dryly again.*
Cynthia:  "Yeah... I guess..."
*Silence returned between us.*
*I nervously tapped my thumb on the table trying to think of something else to talk about. Cynthia seemed to squirm in her seat as she thought of the same thing.*
Cynthia:  "...S-So how was work otherwise?"
*We were back to small talk but I guess it was something.*
You:  "Yeah, it was fine. I was called into the boss’ office for-"
*I stopped myself because I almost said everything without thinking. Would now really be the best time to talk about me being on a business trip? Things were already tense enough?*
*She looked at me curious and I sighed. What good would hiding it do though? Was I supposed to hide it right up to the point where I just left randomly that morning?*
Cynthia:  "What’d you get called into the office for?"
You:  "For a couple of reasons."
You:  "One... They aren’t happy that I was taking time off. You know, when we were sick."
*She gave me an annoyed look.*
Cynthia:  "What else were you supposed to do?"
You:  "Apparently make everyone else sick."
Cynthia:  "And two?"
You:  "Two..."
You:  "...Was because they’re sending me on assignment for a week."
You:  "Remember when Madeline set the coffee pot on fire?"
Cynthia:  "She said there was evidence proving otherwise."
You:  "Well, the person that got burned is the person they usually send so now, I get to go."
Cynthia:  "I’m going to kill her."
You:  "-laughs- You don’t believe she’s innocent anymore."
Cynthia:  "She wasn’t fooling anyone. She definitely did something to start a fire."
You:  "Good to see I wasn’t the only one thinking that."
Cynthia:  "...When do you leave?"
You:  "Next Monday."
*She looked glum as she nodded understandingly.*
*One day after the ultimatum.*
Cynthia:  "What horrible timing is that?"
You:  "I’d quit in a heartbeat if you asked me to stay here though."
*She slowly looked away but I maintained my focus on her.*
Cynthia:  "But there’s a chance I won’t be here."
You:  "And there’s a chance you will. That’s the one I care about more."
*My hands were starting to shake a little so I put them together so I could try and steady myself.*
Cynthia:  "I couldn’t ask you to quit your job for me. It’s not right."
You:  "Then I would do it for me."
*She shook her head.*
Cynthia:  "I wouldn’t want that."
Cynthia:  "As much as you don’t like it, it’s still an important part of your life. You shouldn’t just toss it away. What would you do next? How would you take care of yourself?"
*I started suspecting that she wasn’t directing this at me. These were the questions she was wrestling with herself.*
You:  "So what would you do in my position?"
*She gave me a sad look and remained quiet. She didn’t have an answer. It was obvious and I was only hoping to put her on the spot a bit to get her to confront it.*
*She might not have an answer to her problem, but how would she respond to a similar problem that didn’t involve her?*
*She looked down at the table.*
Cynthia:  "I would go. It’s only a week so it’s not a big deal."
*I nodded. Part of me knew that was what she was going to say, but still, it was a little sad to hear it outloud.*
You:  "Yeah. It’s only a week."
*She stood up and picked up the dishes from the table. While she cleaned up, I didn’t move. I just stayed at the table as if the conversation would be over and the decision final if I dared to move.*
*She saw me sitting there and decided to sit back down again as well. This would probably be our position for the rest of the night at this rate.*
Cynthia:  "Granted... If you were gone for a week... I would miss you."
*I looked up at her a little hopeful.*
Cynthia:  "A week is a long time to go without seeing you. It wouldn’t be a lot of fun."
You:  "A couple of days without seeing you would be too long. Hell, some work days go on too long."
Cynthia:  "Hehe... No need to be greedy, <USER>."
You:  "I can’t help it. I’m a little selfish that way."
You:  "I don’t want to go anywhere and... I don’t want you to go anywhere."
You:  "I think it sounds like you want to stay here."
*She nodded glumly.*
Cynthia:  "I think that’s always been the case, but I just can’t let go of all the people waiting for me to return to my kingdom."
Cynthia:  "It’s not right to abandon them."
You:  "Even after they abandoned you?"
*She sighed.*
Cynthia:  "Well... It’s like that video game we’re playing. With that Randorf guy."
Cynthia:  "Do you remember the first night we played it?"
You:  "I think so."
Cynthia:  "I was sitting in your lap while we played."
You:  "Oh yeah. I definitely remember that but not much else, I’m afraid. I had a very cute girl in my lap after all."
*She smiled sweetly for the first time today.*
Cynthia:  "Hehe... I should do it more often then."
You:  "Yes, please."
*Her smile faded.*
Cynthia:  "Well... You told me what the game was about and I asked a question: What would you do in this situation? Do you remember?"
You:  "I think I do."
*She started sniffing softly as she wiped away a tear.*
Cynthia:  "I’m a failure and coward who sits here in doubt."
You:  "One... You’re not a failure. Not even close. The strength you showed to stay alive and try to rebuild your life...can’t be ignored."
You:  "Who you are right now is because of how hard you worked to get here. Like you said, you thought you had lost everything but you still went on and started a new life from scratch."
Cynthia:  "I did it for selfish reasons though."
You:  "It doesn’t matter why you did it, you never gave up. When given nothing, you turned it into... All this!"
You:  "Even now, you still show such compassion. And the way you put others before yourself... Anyone would be lucky to have you ruling over them!"
You:  "...I know I am."
*She smiled warmly.*
You:  "You have not failed anyone."
*Her eyes watered up a bit and I shifted in my seat a little.*
You:  "And two... I think the doubt is healthy. If you had rushed off to try and fix everything yourself, there’s a good chance you or others could have gotten hurt in the process."
You:  "Taking a step back to think about this clearly isn’t being a coward. It’s trying to make the most responsible decision."
*I started scratching my head.*
You:  "Look... What I said concerning a video game... You can’t take it to heart. It’s easy to make a decision when the situation isn’t real."
You:  "Back then, this wasn’t a real problem. You thought there was no way to go back and were ready to move on to a new life."
You:  "I could have suggested you run through your world with two AK-47s in your hands and shoot anyone that gets in your way and it still would have been as good advice as what I said back then."
You:  "Things are different now. The problem is different now."
You:  "I know the reason I don’t want you to leave and I think it’s the same reason you are hesitant to leave."
Cynthia:  "<USER>."
*I looked back at her.*
You:  "Yeah?"
Cynthia:  "I-I’m going to stay."
*I almost leapt out of my chair at the sound of that but I tried to keep my composure.*
You:  "O-Oh?"
*She nodded.*
Cynthia:  "I think truthfully... When I think about going back, it’s always based on guilt and not based on what I think is right."
Cynthia:  "I’m happy here. Happier than I’ve ever been in my life and... I’m sure the kingdom will be fine without me."
Cynthia:  "It seems they put themselves back together just fine without me..."
You:  "Don’t think that way."
You:  "You didn’t fail them."
You:  "Something happened to make them turn on the royal family and try to kill you. You’re allowed to be a little upset about that and choose yourself over them."
Cynthia:  "Thanks, but... It’s still not right."
Cynthia:  "I’ve made a decision. But it’s going to be awhile before I think it was the right one."
*I nodded understandably. That was the best I could hope for at least.*
Cynthia:  "Will you still be going on the work trip?"
*Ah right. That bullshit.*
You:  "I guess I have to if you’re staying here right?"
You:  "Or did you want me to quit?"
*She gave me a stern look.*
Cynthia:  "You should still go on the trip and do your job. Who knows, maybe good things will come from it."
*I decided to leave out the part where I was considered “the office burden” and they were only trying to get rid of me. It felt like it would ruin the mood.*
You:  "Then I’ll go."
Cynthia:  "We should celebrate the night before. Send you off properly."
You:  "I’d be ok with that. We celebrate you staying and me going. -chuckles-"
Cynthia:  "We’ll word it better than that, <USER>!"
You:  "Ok. But one request..."
Cynthia:  "Yeah?"
You:  "It’s just the two of us that day."
Cynthia:  "Hehe... Of course. I wouldn’t want it any other way."
*She popped out of her chair, looking much lighter than when I first returned home.*
Cynthia:  "What should we do? A fancy dinner?"
Cynthia:  "-gasp- Should we maybe have a little alcohol?"
Cynthia:  "No. You wouldn’t want to be hungover on the first day of your trip."
*Yes, I would.*
You:  "It should be alright. I can hold my alcohol just fine. We can have a drink or two."
Cynthia:  "Then let’s do it! I’ve always wanted to try it here and see what’s different."
*We spent the evening going over the plans for the party and honestly, we could do anything that day and it would be perfect.*
*Seeing her smile again and knowing that she was always going to be here with me was all I needed.*
*A weird noise woke me up and, figuring it was the alarm, I reached over and swatted at it a little.*
Cynthia:  "EE!"
*I opened my eyes and looked over to see my hand resting on her stomach.*
Cynthia:  "What are you swatting me for?"
You:  "Oh sorry. I thought you were the alarm."
Cynthia:  "Hehe... Are you sure you just weren’t trying to get some early morning rubs in?"
You:  "It wasn’t my intention...but..."
*I started rubbing her stomach and she closed her eyes and stretched out with a sigh.*
Cynthia:  "Now THIS is how you start your day."
You:  "So what was that weird noise that woke me up?"
*Her eyes shot open as she glared at me.*
Cynthia:  "It was not weird!"
You:  "Ok then. What was the “normal sounding” noise that woke me up?"
*She sat up and turned away from me.*
Cynthia:  "I sneezed."
*Sneezed? Why would I mistake that for an alarm? How does she sneeze?*
You:  "Sneeze again real quick. I want to see if it’s the sound."
Cynthia:  "I’m not going to sneeze around someone who thinks it sounds weird!"
You:  "Aww. Why not? What if it turns out to be cute? Wouldn’t that be better than weird?"
Cynthia:  "You still said it was weird in the first place so... No."
You:  "Aww. Come on..."
Cynthia:  "Hehe... No, <USER>. No “weird” sneezes for you."
*I exaggerated my disappointment as I turned away from her.*
You:  "That sucks! I was hoping to hear some adorable, little sneezes."
Cynthia:  "You lost your chance. Now get up. It’s a big day today."
*She didn’t need to remind me as I had been watching the calendar like a hawk all week. As much as I didn’t want it to, Sunday had arrived, bringing with it the day that we would need to tell Angelica that Cynthia was staying and our last day together before my work trip.*
*My stomach was in knots over the thought of standing face to face with her again. Her eyes just pierced right through you and made you feel like a 5 year old being scolded for breaking a window. I could only imagine how twisted up Cynthia felt.*
*To her credit, she seemed to be in a good mood most of the time. Though I could only really tell whenever I was at home.*
*She admitted that the days felt a little long and she ended up spending most of her time playing video games to distract herself. We’ve all been there so I tried to make the evenings fun for her.*
*She stretched again and stood up, heading over to her clothes to get dressed.*
Cynthia:  "Are you moving?"
You:  "Maybe... Maybe not..."
*She circled around the bed until she was in front of me and put her hands on her hips.*
Cynthia:  "Well... Do I have to pull you out myself?"
You:  "If you get too close, I’m just going to pull you back into the bed."
*She looked thoughtful for a second before cracking her knuckles.*
Cynthia:  "Challenge accepted."
*She crouched down a little and looked for an angle to strike. I watched her eyes closely, trying to read her next move.*
*Suddenly, she struck, grabbing the blanket and side stepping my attempt to grab her as she threw it off me.*
You:  "Damn it!"
*She giggled and turned her back to me so she could gloat and fold the blanket. I slowly started to creep up to her.*
Cynthia:  "Too easy, <USER>. How do you expect to win with those sl-"
*I seized her from behind and picked her up, slamming her into the bed.*
Cynthia:  "AH!"
You:  "Too easy, Cynthia. You shouldn’t leave your back open like that."
Cynthia:  "Oh no... Whatever will I do..."
*She giggled and cuddled into me a little bit.*
Cynthia:  "I can give you a couple minutes."
You:  "What if I want a couple of hours?"
Cynthia:  "No need to be greedy. You have me all day."
*We lay like that for a little bit and as I was falling asleep again, I felt her slide away from me.*
*She stood up and straightened out her uniform as I sat up and rubbed the sleep out of my eyes.*
Cynthia:  "I’m going to get started with breakfast. Join me when you’re ready."
*She scooted away with a little bounce in her step and I swung my legs off the side of the bed and stretched a bit.*
*“You have me all day,” she had said. Was that going to be enough though? A week away was way too long sounding.*
*I stood up and grabbed some clothes. I heard her mixing something vigorously and giggling to herself. That could only mean one thing.*
*She placed the plate of pancakes in front of me and I poured some syrup over them. I passed her the bottle as she sat down.*
You:  "You’ve gotten much better at making these since you started using a spatula."
Cynthia:  "I hadn’t given up on learning to flip them in the pan. I will master that one day."
You:  "I’m sure you will."
*We began eating slowly as we just enjoyed the sweet taste of the pancakes. Cynthia seemed to be bouncing from excitement in her chair.*
Cynthia:  "Ok. So what’s the action plan for today? We still have to hit up the market and alcohol vendor right?"
You:  "Yeah. There’s a place close to work I like to go to that has a pretty good selection. I was going to run up there."
Cynthia:  "Ok! I can hit up the market for the food then."
You:  "Are you going to be ok on your own?"
Cynthia:  "-giggles- <USER>, I’ve gone to the market plenty of times. I’ll be fine."
*It wasn’t what I meant, but I’ll take it as a sign that she won’t get lonely.*
You:  "Alright. If you want me to come though, just let me know. We can always skip having drinks tonight."
Cynthia:  "-giggles- And miss the opportunity to see you blunder around drunk? Never."
*We all knew who the real lightweight was going to be and I was looking forward to seeing her get drunk.*
Cynthia:  "If you’re worried about me being alone. It’s fine."
Cynthia:  "I’m a little nervous today but... I made my decision."
*I nodded.*
You:  "I know what you mean. We’ll have to face her eventually, won’t we?"
*Cynthia looked away.*
Cynthia:  "Yeah..."
*We finished our pancakes in silence and I gathered up the dishes.*
Cynthia:  "I can handle that, <USER>."
You:  "I got it. You usually have some little chores you like to run before stepping out. I figured you’d want to get started with them so you can head out quicker."
Cynthia:  "I finished everything this week as far as I can remember but I guess I could take another look at the bathroom."
You:  "-chuckles- Who cares about that. You just relax and I’ll take care of this."
Cynthia:  "I guess..."
*I started washing the dishes and she looked a little antsy for a second before scooting off to find something to do. I chuckled again as I finished the task.*
*It was the afternoon by the time we stepped out of the apartment. Cynthia wanted to get a load of clothes in the wash and we hung around until it was done. Finally, it was time to face the world.*
*It was another beautiful Sunday afternoon as the summer continued on. The heat was already starting to feel suffocating and I looked down at Cynthia to see if she was alright.*
*It didn’t seem to bother her as she just simply checked over her list and nodded in approval.*
You:  "Everything checks out?"
Cynthia:  "Yup! I haven’t forgotten anything."
You:  "Let’s head out then."
You:  "What are we having for dinner tonight then?"
Cynthia:  "Hehe... It’s a secret, of course."
You:  "How am I going to know what type of booze I should get if you don’t tell me what we’re having?"
*She looked thoughtful for a second as she tried to decide how much information to give me.*
Cynthia:  "Very well. Hmm..."
Cynthia:  "...Oh! I won’t tell you what it is exactly, but I was thinking of creating a spread. Just a bunch of little finger foods so you can... finger at will."
*I gave her a curious look and she already looked like she had regretted what she said.*
Cynthia:  "Why’d I word it so awkwardly like that?! Oh God!"
*I laughed and shook my head. It looked like her nerves got the best of her there.*
You:  "So hard liquor then?"
Cynthia:  "Please... Anything that will help us forget I said such a horrible line."
You:  "You got it."
*We walked in silence for a bit until the train platform loomed ahead. We would need to part ways from there and part of me was reluctant to go.*
You:  "Are you sure you don’t want me to come with?"
*I already knew the answer but it wouldn’t hurt for me to try again.*
Cynthia:  "I’ll be fine. Embarrassing lines aside, I’m really excited to get all the ingredients together. I’ve been planning this feast all week."
Cynthia:  "And I can’t surprise you if you're watching over my shoulder the whole time, can I?"
You:  "That’s not true. You surprise me constantly."
Cynthia:  "Hehe... Then today I need to make a masterpiece and really throw you off your game."
*She was all fired up and determined. As much as I was worried, it was classic Cynthia walking beside me so I felt a little relieved.*
You:  "-chuckles- Ok. You do what you do best. I’ll get us some good drinks."
Cynthia:  "Just you wait, <USER>. I’m going to make this the tastiest dinner I’ve ever made."
You:  "That’s a tall order. You’ve made some really tasty dishes."
Cynthia:  "They’ll be nothing in comparison!"
*We arrived at the train platform.*
You:  "Then I’ll leave it to you, Super Maid."
*She giggled at the name.*
You:  "Take care and see you when I get back."
*I reluctantly started towards the train.*
Cynthia:  "<USER>."
*I stopped and turned back to her. She looked a little nervous as she took a step forward.*
Cynthia:  "You forgot something."
*She started fumbling through her bag and I walked back over to her with a curious expression. When I was close enough to her, she dropped the act and pulled my face down to hers, kissing me on the cheek delicately.*
*She backed up quickly with a giggle and a blush.*
Cynthia:  "Got you!"
Cynthia:  "See you when you get back."
*She scooted away, trying to hide her blush and I touched my cheek when she had kissed me.*
*My heart pounded in my chest and I could feel a dorky smile spread across my face. Maybe I was more worried about her than I needed to be.*
*With that, it was time for me to do my job. As much as I wasn’t looking forward to the trip downtown, it would be worth it in the end.*
*Having a few drinks in us will definitely help us relax a bit.*
*Cynthia had stopped moving but her heart still raced. The simple act of kissing <USER> on the cheek had almost made her faint.*
*She smacked her cheeks a couple of times to try and focus. <USER> was heading out of town for the week and they had an evening that needed to be perfect.*
*It was important that they established what they meant to each other tonight so Cynthia needed to bring her “A” game.*
*For now though, all that had to wait. It was time for her to begin shopping!*
*She had the list.*
*She had the money.*
*She was pumped up.*
*She was ready to kick some ass.*
*She...wasn’t fooling anyone.*
*She lowered her head and sighed.*
*What was she doing? She just felt silly trying to act like this was some grand quest. Trying to pretend like nothing had changed only caused more issues. She needed to accept that things needed to be handled more seriously moving forward.*
*She pulled out her list and double checked it. It would be better to do this quickly so she could get home to <USER>. The first stop was the butcher.*
*He was a proud man with a smile as broad as his shoulders, Cynthia had found herself always stopping by his shop every time she was in the market.*
*He always seemed to be busy and always seemed to be in a good mood because of it. Today was no different as she waited in line.*
The Butcher:  "Oh, you’re going to love the pork chops today. I found this little farm west of here and the farmer is a good man... Won a lumberjack tournament, he did. You’re not going to find better quality anywhere else. I guarantee it."
*The customer looked over the meat and nodded and Cynthia couldn’t help but smile. Every piece had some story like that to go with it. *
*It wasn’t too long ago Cynthia bought some chicken that had been “raised by a Nobel prize winning scientist that had retired and started up a farm”. Cynthia wasn’t sure what to make of that sentence but the chicken was alright at least.*
*He went through the line of customers quickly until it was Cynthia’s turn. She had already scoped out the counter and knew what she was getting but still, she was prepared to shoot down whatever offer he was preparing.*
The Butcher:  "How are you doing today?"
Cynthia:  "No tha-"
Cynthia:  "-clears throat- I’m fine. Thank you. How are you?"
*He had given her the slip by asking a polite question to start.*
The Butcher:  "-chuckles- I tripped you up this time, didn’t I?"
Cynthia:  "I admit nothing."
The Butcher:  "-laughs- The usual?"
Cynthia:  "Something a little different today actually."
The Butcher:  "Oh? Bring it on."
*She ran through her list and the butcher packed everything up quickly.*
The Butcher:  "Planning something special tonight?"
Cynthia:  "Aren’t I always?"
The Butcher:  "That you are. I hope this one comes out great."
Cynthia:  "Thank you."
*He rang her up at the cash register and Cynthia thanked him before quickly moving out of the way for the next customer.*
*She checked her list as she walked and noticed a small crowd gathering around one of the stalls. A booming voice declared himself the Emperor of Rice and Cynthia smiled. It seemed like the Emperor was up to his tricks again. She detoured a bit to see what was up.*
*A couple of families were around the stall as the man that declared himself the Emperor told them the grand tale of the rice that lay before them.*
*He wasn’t the exaggerator that the Butcher was but he still made the rice seem like it was important. The families looked to be interested but shrinked away in fear as the Emperor smacked their hands with a wooden spoon.*
The Emperor:  "Only the Emperor touches the rice! You will tell him what it is you want and only HE shall retrieve it!"
*Business as usual at that stall. Cynthia didn’t need any rice so she moved on, preparing herself for the produce twins.*
*The two older ladies had always given Cynthia the most grief but today she wanted to try something different.*
*<USER> had dealt with them easily, according to him, and Cynthia begged to learn what the secret was.*
*Apparently, if you keep saying no, they’ll stop and think you’re rude.*
*It was a bold strategy and Cynthia was a little nervous to put it into effect. It wasn’t like her to be rude like that and the thought of people seeing her as rude made her insides twist up into a knot.*
*She couldn’t be stuck at the stalls forever though so she slowly approached them and they had a predatory smile on their faces as they watched her.*
Cynthia:  "(You can do this, Cynthia. You’re a hunter, not prey.)"
Woman in Red:  "Well hello, dear! Welcome back!"
Woman in Blue:  "We certainly hope you’ve been having a lovely day."
Cynthia:  "Yes, thank you."

Woman in Red:  "Oh, you know what would go really well with this?"
Cynthia:  "N-No th-"

*Some things you needed to learn on your own.*

*She still needed to hit up the fishmonger but stopped inside a grocer to get a few more ingredients.*

*With everything else done, it was time to visit the fishmonger. It was her preferred last spot to visit because it was the calmest. *

*She surveyed the market with a smile on her face. As busy as it was, as cramped as it could be and as eccentric as the stall keepers were... It was still a wonderful place and she could see that the shoppers were enjoying themselves as well.*
*The market was always the centre of every city where the common people and the merchants could gather and share news and gossip from around the land.*
*Even with all this world’s technological advancements, Cynthia could still see that those principles still held strong today. It gave her hope that her world and its markets would one day be like this too.*
*Of course, that would assume that they are still running after the revolution. She couldn’t imagine that they were looking so happy these past couple of months as she was sure everything was still a little chaotic.*
*If she could, she would like to restore them to their former glory. Or, at least, try to make them in this world’s image.*
*She scoffed at the thought. “If she could”. She was the queen. If she wanted it to happen it would but... Hadn’t she already rejected that responsibility?*
Fishmonger:  "Are you ok?"

*Seeing Miyu had been a great relief to Cynthia. Though it didn’t help resolve any of her problems, it did comfort her to have a friend like Miyu to confide in. She really needed to find a decision that would leave her feeling satisfied.*

*Cynthia took a deep breath and slowly approached the smaller catgirl.*
*I got off the train and readjusted the bag. The shopping trip had been successful but I had almost dropped the booze on the train after getting bumped.*

Cynthia:  "It had been 2...3 years since they had come to one of my birthday parties so I was really excited to see them happy."
*My heart grew heavy after hearing that but to Cynthia, it seemed to just be the norm for her. She continued without her expression changing whatsoever.*
Cynthia:  "I, of course, put on a great show and they clapped so happily. Afterwards, William was returning the horses to the stable and I wanted to help him. He said it was ok, but he wasn’t going to deny royalty if I pushed, so I did, and the two of us headed for the stable."

*I picked up my drink and took another sip.*

*Cynthia watched <USER> in silence before sighing. It was time. She changed into her dress and gathered up all of her belongings with trembling hands.*

*{i}Dear <USER>,{/i}*
*{i}I hope you’re feeling ok. You had a lot to drink so I’m sure you’re not feeling too great and now you’ve discovered that I’m not here and reading this and confused and I’m sorry.{/i}*

*{i}Please take care of your headache. There’s an old recipe for tea that works great for it. First you want to boil water with a little...{/i}*

Angelica:  "Rule number 1 is the prisoner will not speak when the trial begins. A representative, if one chooses to represent you that is, will speak on your behalf and defend you."
