Madeline:  "Ma’am. We don’t have a lot of time. Can we save the snarkiness for afterwards?"
Angelica:  "Yes, of course. There’ll be plenty to celebrate later."
*She cleared her throat and ran through the rules. It was mostly just simple procedural stuff that wasn’t going to be relevant to me.*
*Like... I don’t think we would be bringing in any barnyard animals as surprise witnesses so we didn’t need to apply for the permit.*
Angelica:  "We will be approaching the throne room soon. I trust I don’t have to mention that you must be on your utmost best behaviour."
You:  "Yes, Mom."
Angelica:  "I have not given birth to you. Please do not call me that."
You:  "Why are we using the throne room? Don’t you guys have a courthouse or something?"
Angelica:  "The courthouse and its...staff have recently been found guilty of crimes against the crown and have been shut down...for the time being, of course."
Angelica:  "We will be returning to an older system where you will be judged by the Queen directly."
*That could work out in my favour actually. Typically, the idea of the monarch serving as the judge could result in some rough outcomes depending on how biased the monarch was to the situation.*
*In our case, it looked like the monarch would be in my favour.*
*It was strange that Angelica would let it play out that way as I was sure she figured that out as well.*
*Did she think she had so much sway over Cynthia that she could easily convince her to sentence me to death?*
*She was in for a rude awakening.*
Angelica:  "Besides, it would be best if we kept your presence a secret from the peasantry. Things are already so tense. It’s best if this was just handled by the nobles."
You:  "So a smaller audience than expected?"
Angelica:  "Yes. As much as I’d love to see you put on display for attempting to s-steal my p-"
Angelica:  "..."
*She cleared her throat.*
Angelica:  "...Your theft. It will not come to pass. As secret as this trial will be, it will still be a momentous one."
*If she only knew.*
*Oh! That reminded me. I reached into my pocket and turned on my phone. Hopefully, the ringer was off so it didn’t make mu-*
*A cheerful little song played and Angelica looked around in confusion. Madeline did her best not to look disappointed in me but there was no stopping it. I nodded in acceptance of my punishment.*
Angelica:  "Did anyone else hear a little noise?"
Madeline:  "Ma’am?"
Angelica:  "I thought I heard something."
Madeline:  "We’re going to be late."
Angelica:  "Yes, well... We can’t have that."
*We reached a big set of doors and Angelica turned to face me, locking eyes with me and giving me a cold, stern look.*
Angelica:  "Before we enter there, tell me something, human."
Angelica:  "Why did you enter this world? Surely, you knew something like this would happen."
You:  "I did and it doesn’t matter. I’m here to be with Cynthia."
Angelica:  "The Queen? Why?"
*Angelica studied me carefully before her expression softened a little. She turned away from me.*
Angelica:  "You’re a fool."
Madeline:  "Yeah, he is."
You:  "I won’t deny it."
*Angelica pushed open the door and we entered the throne room.*
*It was a small assembly for a large room. Each of the nobles were dressed to stand out and had a small entourage surrounding them.*
*And every single eye in the room was looking directly at me.*
*I hadn’t seen this many women look at me in disgust since high school. It was making me feel weirdly nostalgic. *
*I fought the urge to wave at them as I’m sure it would break one of the rules I was only half listening to.*
*I was taken to the centre of the room as Madeline continued to hold me in place, Angelica stood beside the throne as the nobles gathered behind me.*
Angelica:  "Ladies of the realm. Thank you for joining us today. We will be having a short trial to determine the fate of this...human."
*Loud murmuring behind me.*
*Despite the fact that they found the portal between worlds and were studying it, it would seem that the idea of a human was still very alien to them.*
*Maybe that portal and the existence of my world was a bigger secret than I thought.*
Angelica:  "After which, we will begin the official coronation of our next queen, Her Royal Highness, Cynthia Julieanne of House Malineos-Issor."
*Polite clapping with some murmuring from the crowd.*
*That really wasn’t the reception I thought she’d get. Shouldn’t they be happier the royal family had returned?*
*Or maybe that was just the respective proper amount. Which sucked because I really wanted to see one of the nobles just lose it and clap and cheer like a maniac.*
*At that next coronation I attended, if my arms weren’t shackled together, maybe I could be that person. Bring a little pep to the procedure.*
Angelica:  "Afterwards, tea and cakes will be served."
*Oh, that would be nice. I hope I'm around for that!*
Angelica:  "Please allow me to introduce the future queen of Katulinia... Princess Cynthia."
*She bowed and stepped off to the side as Cynthia entered the room. The assembly bowed to her and I felt Madeline’s hand rest on the back of my neck as she pushed me down into a bow as well.*
Madeline:  "(Just go through the motions and don’t hurt your case.)"
*Cynthia nervously took the throne and we all straightened ourselves out while Angelica ran through the introduction procedure.*
*Cynthia stared at me with an expression of nervousness and fear as who knows what raced through her mind.*
*Don’t worry. Madeline and I had a little surprise for everyone.*
Angelica:  "Is there anyone that would speak for the defense?"
*The room was predictably quiet for a second until Madeline stepped forward.*
Madeline:  "Lady Madeline, acting captain of the Order of the Rose, will speak for the defense."
*Surprised murmuring behind me as I nodded to the Klutz.*
Madeline:  "(Let’s get that easy W, baby.)"
You:  "(Easy W?)"
Madeline:  "(Did I say it right? Your world’s sports are fascinating but the culture still eludes me.)"
*As I debated whether to correct her and get her to call it an “easy dubski, bro”, the trial to determine whether I died or not continued on at a relaxed pace.*
Angelica:  "Very well. I speak on behalf of the crown."
Cynthia:  "U-Understood. Let us begin."
*Angelica stepped away from the throne to take up a position to my right. Madeline took up position to my left and the party was about to begin.*
*Cynthia’s mother figure and Cynthia’s sister figure were about to try and convince her on what to do with, well... Me.*
*This would have been way more fun if I was only watching it and not participating in it.*
*Though I did have to admit, I wasn’t as nervous as I thought I’d be. Considering the situation, I thought I would be feeling it a little bit more but I guess my faith in the plan kept me feeling calm.*
Angelica:  "This trial will be to determine the fate of the defendant and his crimes against the Kingdom of Katulinia. Will the defense please state the defendant’s name for the court."
Madeline:  "<USER>."
*Murmurs behind me as people reacted to my name.*
Noble Lady:  "Why would anyone willingly use that name?"
*I just shook my head. It was a great name and everyone knew it.*
Angelica:  "First order of business is the charge of the defendant not being a citizen of our world and trespassing. How does the defense plead to the charge?"
Madeline:  "Guilty as hell."
*Well... We were off to a great start already.*
*I turned to Madeline and she smiled and nodded. I wanted to curse her out but Angelica’s reaction was enough for now.*
Angelica:  "I- ...Ok?"
*Seeing the composed maid look baffled for a moment before trying to regain her usual air of authority was probably why Madeline said what she said.*
*It was similar to the move in the bedroom when she introduced the panties to get her flustered so I could get away.*
*Madeline’s entire strategy might be to use her unpredictableness to win over the crowd and keep control away from Angelica.*
*I turned back to Maddie and nodded. Give her hell, partner.*
*Madeline put herself between the crowd and Cynthia and started to pace with a cocky swagger to her. I think she might have watched a few movies with lawyers in it but I couldn’t tell who she was trying to pretend to be.*
Madeline:  "I think we can all agree that trying to deny this man as human would be just a waste of time."
Madeline:  "I mean, just look at him."
Madeline:  "He lacks any of the basic features you would expect from a person."
*Uh...*
Madeline:  "This man is so tragically generic that it would be obvious to anyone that he wasn’t one of us."
Madeline:  "The fact that he made it this far without being stopped is only a testament to how boring and featureless he is. Nobody would even look twice at this man."
Madeline:  "So why should we waste our time discussing his looks."
*The room was quiet for a second while I scowled at Madeline. Was she really going to take as many cheapshots as she could against me because I couldn’t talk?*
*Well, the joke was on her. My name was awesome. No matter what the crowd thought.*
Angelica:  "...Indeed. So-"
Madeline:  "Instead, let’s focus on the fact that he is here. How he got here is irrelevant because it does not change the fact that he is here. Who is he? What is his goal? That is what we can try to figure out at this moment."
*I think she was just trying to hide her involvement in bringing me over but it seemed to be working.*
Angelica:  "Very well then. Who is this man and why is he here?"
Madeline:  "He...is a fool of the highest caliber."
Madeline:  "He willingly calls himself <USER> so his ability to make decisions is suspect at best."
*I was going to kill her after this.*
*Or at least, I would try. Cynthia was looking less than impressed with this defense so she might be thinking the same thing.*
Madeline:  "In his world, he is nothing but a simple labourer. A lowly, humble peasant doing his best for society."
Madeline:  "To his credit, he is kind and considerate and both myself and our future queen owe him a debt of gratitude for his contribution to our kingdom."
*A surprised murmur from the crowd drew my attention as I started to understand this next strategy she was taking.*
*She was trying to make me appealing to the crowd.*
*I was a scary alien in this world. An unknown. The look in their eyes when I entered the throne room told the story. If I was to walk out of here with my head still attached, the crowd would need to sympathise with this unknown factor.*
*Paint the picture of a fool, someone they can look down at and laugh. Then build them up as a hero to win their hearts.*
*Would it work though? Angelica had a look in her eye as she turned to the crowd that said otherwise.*
Angelica:  "If you are talking about how he forced our queen into servitude. I-"
Madeline:  "You twist words and situations to fit your agenda but you’re overlooking the most important witness to this situation."
*Madeline was getting really into this now. A flair for the dramatic seemed to be creeping into her voice and actions.*
*She turned to Cynthia and gestured towards her with her hand.*
Madeline:  "My queen... How would you describe your time with the defendant in his world?"
*She turned back to Angelica.*
Madeline:  "Or would you not have our queen speak for herself?"
*More murmuring behind me as Madeline was clearly starting to win the crowd.*
*Angelica didn’t say anything but her jaw tightened in anger and she turned to Cynthia to hear her reply.*
*Cynthia rose and all eyes immediately snapped to her.*
Cynthia:  "I can not begin to describe how grateful I am to this man. When I had nowhere to turn, he kept me safe. He took care of me and asked for nothing in return."
Cynthia:  "The servitude Angelica speaks of was my own idea. I merely wished to repay him a small amount for everything he had done."
*I smiled up at her. She didn’t owe me anything. It was the other way around. She changed my life for the better and there was nothing I could do to repay her for that.*
*Cynthia sat back down and the crowd talked amongst themselves briefly. Madeline turned back to the crowd and their attention snapped to her.*
Madeline:  "Who is this man?"
Madeline:  "A simple man. An ally. One who was with us in our darkest hour. One who kept our queen safe. A shining knight protecting us fo-"
*Angelica laughed in interruption of Madeline.*
Angelica:  "Your theatrics are getting the better of you, Lady Madeline. But please, stick to the facts. Tell the crowd the other crimes in which this man was imprisoned for. At your own hands, I may add."
Madeline:  "The crime is a false accusation so I will not bring it up."
Angelica:  "A false accusation you say?"
*She sighed. It seemed like she caught the dramatic bug too. Was I in court or was I in a high school play? It was getting really hard to tell the difference.*
Angelica:  "Noble ladies of the court... This “shining knight”, as the {i}acting{/i} captain so elegantly put it, is not the person she wants you to believe."
Angelica:  "Upon his arrest, he was found in my room... Raiding my u-"
*She blushed as she still seemed incapable of saying the word.*
Angelica:  "...The drawer with my unmentionables in them."
*You could feel the energy of the crowd shift with that reveal. The goodwill Madeline had been trying to build was quickly evaporating and I hoped she had a plan for that.*
Angelica:  "Does that sound like something someone of upstanding moral fiber would do?"
Angelica:  "Is it even unreasonable to suspect that he may have done...unsavory things to our very queen during her time there?"
Angelica:  "Or would yo-"
*She stopped because she could see Madeline laughing to herself.*
Angelica:  "What are you laughing about now?"
Madeline:  "I was just thinking about how adorable your underwear were."
*Angelica froze up and Madeline turned to the crowd.*
Madeline:  "You would think she would wear those big long ones, but they’re actually really cute. To think, underneath all that are an adorable pair of white pa-"
Angelica:  "Silence!"
*Madeline turned back to Angelica and studied her seriously.*
Madeline:  "Tell me... Was he alone in the room or was there someone else there?"
Angelica:  "You were there. You made the arrest."
*Madeline focused back on the crowd.*
Madeline:  "If the noble ladies would be so kind. Tell me, how many times have you found me snooping around in your rooms while you were here."
Noble Lady:  "Too many times."
Madeline:  "Oh, don’t be that way. Your underwear are adorable too."
*Some snickering from the other nobles as the first one that spoke up grumbled to herself.*
*I think it’s pretty safe to assume that if I admitted to stealing the panties, it would be a little more believable than say... Someone from another world traveling across the entire country to raid a single drawer...*
*Some murmurs in agreement as Angelica just rolled her eyes.*
Angelica:  "If it wasn’t him that was stealing the panties but you, then why were you both in my room?"
Madeline:  "Why indeed..."
*She had the crowd’s full attention.*
Madeline:  "It’s time we answered the second question I presented: Why is this man here? The answer is simple."
Madeline:  "He’s here under orders from me to investigate the cause of the revolution."
*Someone from the crowd gasped as we all looked in shock at Madeline. Did she really just admit that she brought me across the worlds?*
Angelica:  "Are you saying that you breached protocol and brought a foreign species into our land."
*Madeline put on her goofy little smile.*
Madeline:  "You expect anything less from me?"
*She cleared her throat.*
Madeline:  "The human world’s technological advancements are staggering compared to ours. It’s because of this vast difference that we were able to get a recording of a certain conversation between Angelica and another maid."
Madeline:  "I will play this conversation and I suspect everyone will quickly understand what we were doing in your room."
*Angelica looked confused as Madeline headed over to me.*
*I fumbled around in my pocket and handed her the phone. She looked down at it and shook her head.*
Madeline:  "(How do you...?)"
*I showed her how to get to the video.*
Madeline:  "(So archaic.)"
You:  "(It’s a good phone!)"
Madeline:  "(The accused can’t talk.)"
*She left and I shook my head at her back.*
*She held my phone up so everyone could see.*
Madeline:  "This is a cell phone. In the human world, it is used for many things, such as ordering food to your house, playing games and, on occasion, sending scripts and letters to those that are far away."
*That was probably the weirdest way I’d heard cell phones described but it got some interested whispers behind me.*
Madeline:  "It has another interesting feature that lets you capture still images of time and record messages from the past."
Madeline:  "Let me know if you recognise the voice or not."
*She smiled smugly as she hit the play button and Angelica’s orders to the maid were played in front of the entire royal court.*
*The stillness of everyone was eerie as my phone had captured the entire attention of the room.*
*Most of them didn’t know what they were looking at or what was going on but one of them did. Perhaps, the most important in the room understood what this meant.*
*She rose up and stepped forward, a fearful look on her face as she stared down Angelica.*
Cynthia:  "What did you do?"
*Angelica cleared her throat, trying to maintain her composure as the weight of everyone’s stares beared down on her.*
Angelica:  "Your Highness... I don’t know what device Lady Madeline is trying to present here, but it shouldn’t be seen as anything more than simply trickery or illusions for children."
Angelica:  "Lady Madeline could simply have bought an oddity at a store and be doing ventriloquism to mimic my voice. Given her track record, it would hardly be surprising."
*I tried to picture the Klutz being able to do ventriloquism and I shuddered. I couldn’t picture anything more annoying at work than her and 2 of the items on my desk asking me questions all day long.*
*Madeline made a show of sighing in exasperation, but before she could say anything, Cynthia was already looking at her with her hand stretched out.*
Cynthia:  "Hand me the phone, Maddie."
*Madeline went up to the throne and handed it to her. Cynthia investigated it and sighed.*
Cynthia:  "This is <USER>’s phone alright. Maddie, go stand next to Angelica for a moment and I’ll play the message again."
*Madeline did as she was instructed and Cynthia played the message again.*
*During the second time, I could feel a savage energy building up from the crowd behind me. The words weren’t any easier to hear the second time and the nobles were starting to want some answers.*
Cynthia:  "Madeline certainly has her talents, but the ability to throw her voice across the room while performing ventriloquism isn’t one of them."
Cynthia:  "My time in the human’s world has taught me what this device is and what it can do. I can confirm that the message on this phone is accurate and not trickery. "
Cynthia:  "Angelica, what is the meaning of this order?"
*Angelica was reeling at this moment, trying desperately to find a way to play it off.*
Angelica:  "I believe people are simply misunderstanding the message that has been played."
Madeline:  "HA! There’s not much to misunderstand, I’m afraid."
Madeline:  "“Move on to Plan B”. You “hope the peasants are easier to handle THIS TIME.” “Replace one of the Duchess’ servants.”"
Madeline:  "Nothing about this message leaves room for misunderstanding."
Madeline:  "In fact, let’s ask the Duchess herself about something."
*She turned to the crowd and Angelica just scowled at her back.*
Madeline:  "Is it not true that I stopped by your quarters last night with a message."
Duchess:  "Yeah, you did."
Madeline:  "Did you have any issues yesterday?"
Duchess:  "Yeah. You said to watch our servants and to keep the maids at bay. I thought it was a strange request but we did have a run in with a maid late at night."
Duchess:  "We sent her away but it was reported that she was rather...insistent that she be allowed to enter. I see why now."
Duchess:  "Did you really think that would work, maid? My servants are not just nameless, faceless drones to me. Your girl would have been discovered immediately."
*Angelica smirked.*
Angelica:  "It seems like I miscalculated your defenses."
*Angelica turned to Madeline, a little more confident in herself now.*
Angelica:  "Seeing as nothing happened. Your little “message from the past” has little relevance to our current trial."
Angelica:  "Certainly. I tried to replace one of the Duchess of Duncall Vale’s servants with my own but it failed before any crime could be committed. It was just another day of court intrigue as far as we are all concerned."
Angelica:  "After all, how long will I have to wait before the Duchess retaliates with her own."
Duchess:  "It won’t be long, maid. And it won’t be such underhanded tactics. I’ll be talking to you face to face."
*Wow. I was starting to see why Madeline said prison was the safest place for me to be last night.*
Angelica:  "Are you done, Lady Madeline? Perhaps we can get to the verdict of this human."
Madeline:  "We’re not there yet. After all, it was so nice of you to admit that the orders were yours because you said some interesting stuff."
*The confidence left Angelica’s face as she watched Madeline closely.*
Madeline:  "Our investigation uncovered more interesting information you will need to clarify for us."
Madeline:  "Perhaps you are familiar with... The Angel of Duncall Vale?"
*Angelica’s face didn’t change but there was some heated murmuring behind me where the Duchess was.*
Madeline:  "We found an eyewitness who had told us a fair lot about this figure and he also told us about how the attack in the castle went. Very interesting tale."
Duchess:  "Where is this eyewitness now?"
Madeline:  "Somewhere safe, we can only hope. Eyewitnesses have been disappearing very quickly as of late. Everytime I get close to one, they end up dead. Thankfully, we found one while he was still alive."
Madeline:  "Hopefully, he still is."
*She side eyed Angelica who showed nothing.*
Madeline:  "The eyewitness told us of the rise and sudden disappearance of the Angel of Duncall Vale. An anti-noble revolution was incited out that way but the Duchess was able to put it down quickly. Am I correct?"
Duchess:  "We heard reports of suspicious gathers and were able to infiltrate the meetings, learning the time and place of the riots and stop it before it could get out of control."
Duchess:  "We arrested everyone but never found the so-called “Angel” they talked about."
Madeline:  "Despite your arrests, a few of the conspirators fled, arriving in the capital where they continued to spread the “Angel’s” message. Soon, an anti-monarchy revolution had broken out."
Madeline:  "I was in the castle and can confirm a little about the eyewitness story. He said that the mob had met the maids making a last stand and stopped when Angelica talked to their leaders."
Madeline:  "I can confirm that happened as we used it as the chance to get Princess Cynthia to safety."
Angelica:  "Yes. We were unarmed and simply asked the people to show us mercy and talk it out. The people may be a little misguided, but they still showed they were capable of compassion and we stopped the fighting."
Madeline:  "That’s not what he heard."
Madeline:  "He was right there in the crowd and he heard it as clear as day. You told them you were a part of their conspiracy and that you wished to speak with the leaders. They disbanded immediately."
*Did he? I was trying to think back to everything he said, but I couldn’t remember. Was she calling a bluff here to get Angelica to bite?*
Madeline:  "What did you tell them? Did you admit to being the Angel of Duncall Vale?"
*Angelica rolled her eyes and sighed.*
Angelica:  "Who was this eyewitness? Are we to believe that some random citizen would be able to identify me out of a crowd."
Madeline:  "The eyewitness worked in the castle. He was William, the stable master. He knows you pretty well and assured us that it was you calling the shots."
*Angelica sighed again.*
Angelica:  "Without this witness here today, this is all a lot of fantasy."
Madeline:  "Tell me this then. If you are not the Angel of Duncall Vale, what did you mean by saying that you “hope the peasants will be easier to handle this time.” Have you already incited a riot somewhere before?"
Madeline:  "Who was the “she” who quelled the riot last time? Would it happen to be the Duchess of Duncall Vale, the same person whose servant you were trying to replace?"
Madeline:  "Shall we play the recording of your orders again? The orders you’ve already admitted are yours and just part of the usual “court intrigue”?"
*The room was silent as Angelica stared a hole through Madeline.*
*This was it, wasn’t it?*
*She was trapped. Madeline had set everything up and Angelica had nowhere to move. All she could do is deny and clam up, but how would that be handled in this system of justice?*
*Would they care enough about the evidence to delay and search for the eyewitness or would mob justice take over the already tense crowd?*
*The room was eerily quiet.*
*Angelica and Madeline didn’t move as they stared death at each other, but Cynthia stood up from the throne and approached them, finally breaking their staredown.*
*She looked like a wreck, a mixture of fear and anger. She probably had her own suspicions of Angelica’s story but had pushed them out of her head out of respect to the maid that raised her.*
*Now, though, there was information that couldn’t be ignored.*
*Cynthia looked to Madeline first.*
Cynthia:  "Is everything you said true?"
Madeline:  "Yes. I swear it."
*She studied Madeline as it seemed the two of them were having a telepathic conversation.*
*Which one was she going to trust?*
*She turned to Angelica.*
Cynthia:  "What do you say to that?"
Angelica:  "Your Highness, I think-"
*Cynthia raised her hand and Angelica stopped. The balance of power between them shifted as Cynthia gave her a serious look.*
Cynthia:  "I order you to tell the truth. If you have any integrity or respect for House Malineos-Issor and those you serve, you will not lie to me."
*Angelica closed her eyes and bowed her head.*
Angelica:  "Yes, Your Highness."
*She took a moment to collect herself before raising her head again.*
Angelica:  "The Duchess of Duncall Vale had simply grown too powerful. Her influence on the Kingdom was becoming too much to ignore. It would only be a matter of time before she rose up against our Queen."
Duchess:  "I, and my family, have been nothing but loyal to the crown."
Angelica:  "Your actions were starting to overstep your authority as you acted without the Queen’s approval."
Duchess:  "I was acting in my capacity as Marshal as the Queen instructed."
Cynthia:  "Enough. Both of you."
*The room was quiet again.*
Cynthia:  "Continue."
Angelica:  "Yes, Your Highness."
Angelica:  "My plan was to incite a riot in her city in hopes of shaming her in the eyes of the other nobles."
Angelica:  "She thwarted my attempt but I underestimated the peasant’s reactions to my agenda."
Angelica:  "The “Angel of Duncall Vale” name was nothing more than a fervent few’s fever dream as they twisted my words of anti-authority into a message of anti-monarchy. It was only too late that I realised the message had infected the capital."
Angelica:  "I sent a message out for aid, but it would be too late. The peasants revolted and stormed the castle."
Angelica:  "Though indirectly, my actions resulted in the death of the Queen and nearly cost our dear Princess her life."
Angelica:  "I was able to get the peasants to listen to me by identifying myself as the Angel of Duncall Vale and held a meeting with them under the guise of occupying the capital. "
Angelica:  "They took the bait and as the noble armies arrived, we made the move to eliminate the leaders of the revolution to end the situation quickly."
Angelica:  "Of course, the first one to arrive was the Duchess of Duncall Vale who used the situation to seize control of the capital. "
Angelica:  "It was clear that she was planning on usurping the throne and further actions needed to be taken, especially if our Princess were to return."
Angelica:  "Who knows what she would try to do to prevent you from taking power. I wouldn’t allow something like that to happen to you. Not after I..."
*She bit her lip and looked away but she didn’t need to say anymore. The crowd behind me was getting more heated as they talked amongst themselves.*
Duchess:  "Who ordered you to do all this?"
Angelica:  "Nobody. It was all my own actions."
Noble Lady:  "And we’re supposed to believe that?!"
Angelica:  "Prove otherwise. I’ve been ordered to speak the truth and that’s what I’ve done."
Noble Lady:  "You’ve betrayed us all is what you’ve done!"
*The crowd was starting to get really antsy as they hurled more accusations at Angelica. All the tension that had been building up in them now finally had a release and it would be only a matter of time before it got out of hand.*
*With the truth out in the open, I expected Madeline to gloat and cheer but she was eerily quiet and watching Cynthia closely.*
*I looked up to her too and saw she was frozen after hearing everything. I tried to get Madeline’s attention so she would come closer.*
You:  "Everything ok, partner?"
Madeline:  "I don’t know... It wasn’t easy to hear."
*She looked up to Cynthia again.*
Madeline:  "I can’t imagine what’s going through her mind right now."
*The crowd was starting to get really loud and a couple of the guards were moving to try and calm down the nobles.*
You:  "I think you should get Angelica out of here before this mob gets out of hand."
You:  "Who knows what will happen if they get a hold of her."
*Madeline looked over the crowd and nodded.*
Madeline:  "Yeah, you’re right."
Madeline:  "Don’t do anything stupid while I’m gone."
*She hurried over to Angelica and whispered in her ear. Angelica was already looking a little defeated and after hearing from Madeline, she could only nod in acceptance.*
*She looked to Cynthia one last time and they made eye contact. “I’m sorry,” Angelica mouthed to her as Cynthia struggled to hold back her tears.*
*Madeline escorted Angelica out of the throne room, waving off the remaining guards and directing them to the crowd.*
*They hurried to stop the nobles while Angelica left with her head held high. I had no idea what to make of that woman.*
*After hearing her story... I had been right. There was no grand conspiracy or anything. It was simply the story of a maid trying to clean up her own mess.*
*She was much too serious and believed too much in her responsibility to just simply admit she was wrong. She needed to correct it and every time she did, it made it worse.*
*In her mind, she was probably doing it all for Cynthia. She didn’t want anybody to interfere or ruin the world for Cynthia. She would eliminate everyone that was a threat to her.*
*Whether it be the Duchess of Duncall Vale or simply... Me.*
*I think at the end of the day... Angelica was nothing more than just an overprotective mother watching over her child. *
*And that overprotectiveness had crossed a line and now, neither of them could go back to normal after that.*
*Angelica was guilty... And Cynthia was now alone.*
*She slowly made her way over to the throne to sit down. From my position, I could see her trembling and trying to calm herself down. *
*She needed to know that she wasn’t completely alone, she had so many people that supported her.*
*I wanted to rush over to her, but the weight of the chains and the chaos around me, reminded me of my place right now.*
*Without Madeline here as my backup, I would be at the mercy of the normal guards and if I made a move for their queen, who knew how they would interpret it. *
*The last thing I wanted to do was cause Cynthia more grief by putting myself in danger.*
*She looked up and we locked eyes. I tried to nod and give her some reassurance that I was here for her, but she shakily looked away.*
*What was going to happen next?*
*It took 10 minutes for things to calm down again. In that time, Cynthia still looked a little lost, but she was able to compose herself a little and stand up again.*
*The crowd was on edge but gave her their full attention. With Angelica removed and Madeline not around... It was just Cynthia up there to make the decision.*
*What would be my fate?*
*What would be hers?*
*What was the fate of the kingdom or the fate of our relationship now?*
*With the trial over, it was time to answer those questions.*
*She looked at me and I smiled at her softly. My heart pounded in my chest as I tried my best to remain calm.*
*I believe in you, Cynthia. Make the right choice.*
Cynthia:  "F-Fair noble ladies of our great Kingdom. This trial has opened our eyes to many new things and it’s fair to say it’s time to give my ruling on the various crimes that were commited."
Cynthia:  "First, House Malineos-Issor’s head maid, Angelica, is found guilty of crimes including conspricy, murder and t-treason."
Cynthia:  "Her actions and opinions against the great duchy of Duncall Vale do not reflect the opinions of House Malineos-Issor and we apologise for this insult against you."
Cynthia:  "You have been nothing but loyal to my mother for years and if you have a request for compensation, pl-"
Duchess:  "No compensation is needed, Your Highness. Her being brought to justice is all we needed."
Cynthia:  "Thank you."
*The nobles lightly chattered in agreement to the ruling.*
Cynthia:  "Second order of business will refer to Lady Madeline’s violation of the sacred protocol by bringing a human across into our world."
Cynthia:  "In light of her recent service to the Kingdom by exposing the conspiracy behind the revolution, I rule that no formal charges be pressed in the case and, instead, she be handed a disciplinary action of my choosing later."
*Translation: Cynthia was upset that Madeline put me in danger and was going to make her pay for it later. Poor girl.*
*The nobles seemed to agree with the ruling though as I was sure “Madeline” and “disciplinary action” were often used together and just par for the course at this point.*
Cynthia:  "Which brings us to the fate of the human."
Cynthia:  "Due to his service to the crown in providing me with a safe haven during my hiding and his assistance of Lady Madeline during her investigation, I rule that his case be dismissed and overrule the earlier guilty plea."
Cynthia:  "It wouldn’t be right to execute someone for their service to our cause. Instead..."
Cynthia:  "...He will be exiled from our lands immediately."
*My heart dropped and the weight of the chains nearly pulled me to the ground with it.*
Cynthia:  "The existence of the human’s world must continue to remain secret as we must focus on protecting ourselves during this turbulent time."
*I just felt numb all over. I was getting kicked out? I was never going to get to see Cynthia again?*
*The nobles seemed to agree with the ruling and I wanted to sneer at them but they might call for me to be executed again if I did that.*
*Cynthia continued to look a little nervous as she fidgeted in front of everyone.*
*I kept my head down as Cynthia dismissed the guard. The guard was insistent on staying and it took Madeline’s return before the guard would finally leave us.*
Cynthia:  "How is she?"
Madeline:  "Hasn’t said a word."
Madeline:  "You know... I was kind of hoping to feel better after finally getting justice for the others..."
Madeline:  "I’m not sure how to feel right now."
Cynthia:  "I know what you mean. I didn’t want to believe it when you said everything... But then she admitted it."
Cynthia:  "It just doesn’t feel real."
Madeline:  "I’m sorry it had to go down that way."
*Cynthia shook her head.*
Cynthia:  "It was the only way. Whether she intended to or not, she was guilty."
Cynthia:  "I just wish I felt something right now instead of...whatever this is."
Madeline:  "You’re just numb with shock. Give yourself some time."
Cynthia:  "Yeah. I’m sure it will all hit me tonight. I’ll talk to her after this and maybe hear her side a little more."
Madeline:  "In the meantime, what’s up with this guy?"
*She nudged into me and I looked up at her.*
You:  "I’ve been exiled."
Madeline:  "Tough break. I guess that means we won."
Cynthia:  "I had to declare a no contest because of your guilty plea at the beginning."
Madeline:  "What? I was hoping to pad my stats a little and go down as the biggest upset in history."
Madeline:  "Well, I guess that’s better than being executed."
